[CASE:LEGAL_ETHICS_BAR_CAPTURE]
[PRIMARY_Q:MORAL LEGIBILITY]
Legal ethics under bar association capture
  [ENCOUNTER-DEPENDENT: N/A -- not ART type]
  S:3 [E3: enforcement pattern documented across jurisdictions,
    billing ethics literature, unauthorized practice case law
    extensive; M3: mechanism clean, divergence pattern holds
    cross-jurisdictionally; F3: harm differential alternative
    examined and falsified by within-population asymmetry]
  Primary Q: MORAL LEGIBILITY [instrument functional; axis
    captured; ACWL failure mode -- primary confirmation case
    for WIP 15]
  Secondary Q: GENUINE AXIS [absent at institutional level --
    axis installed by bar association structure, not generated
    internally by members]; CORRECTION CAPACITY [fires correctly
    within captured reference frame; misdirected by axis not
    by instrument failure]; MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY [surface
    honest to captured standard -- Reference Drift at ethical
    scale]
  Proof for: WIP 15 MORAL LEGIBILITY -- ACWL failure mode
    primary confirmation case. Bar association ethics
    enforcement demonstrates instrument functional [members
    can derive ethical conclusions from outputs] while axis
    determining which outputs matter is owned by association
    with structural interests diverging from client substrate.
  INSTRUMENT FUNCTIONALITY [ML present]:
    Practicing attorneys operate under case outcome system.
    Causal chain from action to outcome traceable at ethical
    resolution -- client harmed, client served, procedure
    followed or violated all legible to instrument.
    Confirmation: legal malpractice doctrine functions;
    attorneys report colleagues, withdraw from representation,
    refuse instructions based on output readings. Instrument
    demonstrably present and operational in population.
  AXIS CAPTURE [GA absent at institutional level]:
    Bar association sets reference standard for what counts
    as ethical output. Members' instrument fires within the
    reference frame the association provides. Axis
    determining which outputs matter is owned by association
    with structural interests in: market entry restriction,
    billing rate maintenance, liability protection, member
    density control. Axis installation mechanism: ethics
    rules drafted by association bodies; enforcement
    controlled by association disciplinary apparatus;
    professional identity formation occurs inside association
    reference frame.
  DIVERGENCE PATTERN [ACWL tell]:
    Unauthorized practice enforcement: maximum. Non-lawyers
    providing accurate legal information trigger aggressive
    response even where client outcomes are good. Instrument
    could read "client served, no harm" -- axis points to
    market protection instead.
    Billing fraud enforcement: minimum relative to harm.
    Six-minute increment billing for thirty-second work,
    double-billing, task padding -- documented extraction
    from clients. Instrument can read the harm. Enforcement
    fires softly because axis owner's interest [member
    protection] overrides instrument reading.
    Pattern holds cross-jurisdictionally. Tracks association
    structural interest consistently.
  ACWL SIGNATURE [externally indistinguishable]:
    From outside: ethics enforcement active, instrument
    appears functional, conclusions appear grounded in
    output readings. Tell only visible at divergence moment
    -- when axis owner's interest conflicts with client
    substrate, enforcement tracks owner's interest.
    Misidentification risk: reads as Moral Capture rather
    than ACWL. Intervention difference is load-bearing:
    Moral Capture = liberate instrument from owner. ACWL =
    examine and revise axis, not instrument. Liberating
    instrument while leaving reference standard intact
    restores nothing -- axis is the problem.
  FALSIFICATION CONDITIONS:
    ACWL reading falsified if: enforcement asymmetry
    [unauthorized practice vs billing fraud] explained by
    harm differential rather than axis capture.
    Stress-tested v20: harm differential requires licensed
    practice to be systematically safer than unlicensed.
    Evidence does not support this. Within-population
    asymmetry [licensed billing fraud underenforced vs
    licensed unauthorized assistance overenforced] falsifies
    harm differential independently of unlicensed comparison.
    Alternative examined; does not hold. F3 confirmed.
    ML presence falsified if: attorneys cannot derive
    ethical conclusions from output data independently
    of association guidance. Falsified by malpractice
    doctrine function and colleague-reporting behavior.
  Cross-ref: WIP 15 [MORAL LEGIBILITY -- primary
    confirmation case]; ACWL [failure mode definition];
    GENUINE AXIS [absent at institutional level]; REFERENCE
    DRIFT [RI failure -- surface honest to captured
    standard]; CORRECTION CAPACITY [fires within captured
    frame]; MSC [not primary mechanism -- axis capture not
    surface capture; distinction load-bearing for
    intervention]; INTERVENTION DIVERGENCE [WIP 15].

[CASE:VOLATILE_HOME_ICD_FC]
[PRIMARY_Q:FORMATION CAPTURE]
Volatile home formation environment [real -- ACE research program;
  Felitti et al. 1998, Kaiser Permanente, n=17,421; van der Kolk
  2014 clinical synthesis; Eisenberg, Cumberland & Spinrad 1998
  parental emotion socialization]
  Type: Primary [S:3]
  Proof for: ICD Vector B [volatile-home vigilance as SR deprivation
    pathway] and FC SELF-MSC REDIRECT sub-mechanism [formation-window
    instrument misdirection from internal to external monitoring].
  MECHANISM:
    Unpredictable or volatile formation environment [parental mood
    volatility, high-consequence unpredictability, chronic threat
    signals] installs a continuous external-monitoring requirement
    during the Nascent window when axis formation and internal
    self-reading capacity would otherwise develop [SDT: integrated
    regulation formation window]. Two simultaneous effects:
    [1] ICD VECTOR B: SR deprivation via sustained hypervigilance.
      Vigilance state maintains chronic arousal; substrate
      restoration is structurally blocked while the monitoring
      apparatus runs. Distinct from Vector A [input-seeking /
      notification ICD] in origin [threat environment vs.
      input architecture] and behavioral residue [threat-seeking
      vs. anxiety-seeking].
    [2] SELF-MSC REDIRECT: instrument that should develop to read
      internal state [emotions, needs, preferences] is instead
      trained onto external threat signals. The self-reading
      surface is captured by the formation environment's
      monitoring requirement. Post-formation: apparatus runs
      without original threat. Adult signature: automatic
      room-scanning, detailed external state-reading, poor
      internal state access, anxiety in low-threat environments
      [instrument generates signal when trained target is absent].
  EVIDENCE:
    ACE study [Felitti et al. 1998]: documents hypervigilance
      and somatic dysregulation outcomes across ACE score levels
      in large-N, multi-decade, independently replicated research.
      Mechanism trace: ACE exposure → hypervigilance → chronic
      physiological arousal → somatic outcome cluster.
    van der Kolk 2014 [clinical synthesis]: somatic residue of
      childhood threat environments includes the vigilance apparatus
      and its post-formation persistence in safe environments.
    Eisenberg et al. 1998: parental emotion socialization confirms
      the formation-window pathway -- controlling or volatile
      emotional environments during development produce measurable
      differences in self-regulatory capacity and internal state
      access. Peer-reviewed; directly confirms formation pathway.
    SDT [CASE:SDT_CHILD_INTERNALIZATION]: controlling formation
      environments produce introjection rather than integration --
      the Nascent → Axial transition fails or is distorted when
      the environment requires external-compliance orientation.
  Weapons active: ICD [Vector B]; FORMATION CAPTURE [SELF-MSC
    REDIRECT sub-mechanism].
  Cross-ref: ICD [Vector B definition]; FORMATION CAPTURE
    [SELF-MSC REDIRECT]; [CASE:SDT_CHILD_INTERNALIZATION]
    [formation pathway confirmation]; WIP 36 [CLOSED v25].

[CASE:PL480_US_FOOD_AID]
[PRIMARY_Q:EXTRACTION]
US PL 480 Food Aid Program [real -- 1954-present; primary analysis
  period 1954-2005; Barrett & Maxwell 2005, 2007]
  Type: Primary [S:3]
  Proof for: MANUFACTURED DEPENDENCE [Section Two ~5477; first
    primary case -- five-condition instrument run].
  FIVE-CONDITION INSTRUMENT RUN [v23]:
    [1] CAPACITY-BUILDING WAS ACHIEVABLE: yes. PL480 recipient
      countries were not in structurally permanent food deficit --
      many had functional local agricultural sectors at program
      inception [Ethiopia, sub-Saharan Africa broadly]. A path to
      reduced food insecurity through local production development
      existed and was achievable. Condition passes.
    [2] NO STATED EXIT CONDITION: confirmed. PL480 was designed
      from the outset around agricultural surplus disposal and
      trade promotion [Barrett & Maxwell 2005 explicitly: "donor
      country agricultural surplus disposal, trade promotion and
      other motives still sharply constrain what can be
      accomplished"]. No metric defining when recipient food
      insecurity would be resolved and the program's presence
      unnecessary was built into the program's design. The
      program's success metric was shipment volume, not recipient
      capacity growth. Condition passes.
    [3] HELPER INTEREST MISALIGNED WITH RESOLUTION: confirmed.
      Barrett & Maxwell document that PL480 served US agricultural
      surplus disposal interests, maritime transport sector
      interests [US-flagged vessel requirements], and PVO
      operational funding interests [monetization for cash].
      Resolution of recipient food insecurity would decrease
      demand for all three. Condition passes cleanly -- this is
      the strongest condition in this case, with independent
      academic confirmation.
    [4] LOCAL CAPACITY DISPLACED OR UNDERCUT: partial. The
      evidence is mixed at national scale [Barrett 2006 notes
      "no persistent negative effects at national level"] but
      documented at local/regional scale in specific contexts
      [market flooding in poorly-integrated local markets;
      monetized aid depressing prices received by local
      producers]. Not cleanly confirmed at case-wide level --
      holds in some contexts, not all. Condition passes
      PARTIALLY; should be noted as contested rather than
      confirmed.
    [5] RECIPIENT VOICE ON DEPENDENCE VS EXIT NOT SOUGHT OR NOT
      WEIGHTED: confirmed structurally. PL480 program design was
      determined by US congressional and agricultural interests,
      not by recipient country populations' preferences between
      continued support and capacity-building path. No formal
      mechanism for recipient voice on program design or exit
      existed within the program's structure. Condition passes.
  INSTRUMENT RESULT: four of five conditions pass cleanly;
    condition [4] passes only partially [context-dependent; not
    universal]. Four clean passes plus one partial is strong
    evidence for the mechanism while preserving condition [4] as
    a contested empirical question in this domain.
  T3 Medium: well-documented in development economics literature
    [Barrett & Maxwell peer-reviewed work; WTO Doha Round debates;
    multiple independent researchers]; primary mechanism [condition 3]
    confirmed across independent academic sources. Condition [4]
    contested across independent sources -- genuine finding about
    mechanism distribution, not a T3 limitation. S:3.
  Cross-ref: [REF:PL480_US_FOOD_AID]; MANUFACTURED DEPENDENCE
    [Section Two ~5477]; EXTRACTION [Manufactured Dependence
    subtype]; [CASE:WORLD_BANK_STRUCTURAL_ADJUSTMENT] [resolves
    condition [4] ambiguity]; [CASE:IPV_ECONOMIC_EMPOWERMENT_RCT]
    [contrast -- legitimate substrate-building]; WIP 32 [staging
    origin v25; migrated from wip archive v25].

  Arc: George Floyd killing May 25 2020 -> mass protests + BLM surge ->
    corporate/brand formation in consumer-facing discourse faces silence-
    is-complicity frame -> each public actor must match or exceed prior
    solidarity gestures [statements -> black squares -> timed silences ->
    escalating donation pledges] -> de-escalation or silence reads as
    betrayal; ratchet distributed without central coordinator.

  FORMATION [anti-chud wedge cluster sibling]:
    Corporate brands, media platforms, music industry [#TheShowMustBePaused
    -> Blackout Tuesday Jun 2 2020], institutions in same visibility
    field -- membership = reputational standing with consumers, employees,
    and peer brands.

  MECHANISM TRACE [behavioral record]:
    [1] OBLIGATION CHAIN INSTALLED: Netflix May 30 2020 -- "To be silent
      is to be complicit. Black lives matter." [CSMonitor; widely
      retweeted]. Ben & Jerry's Jun 2020 -- "Silence is NOT an Option"
      [brand activism literature]. Silence reframed as active complicity
      within formation.
    [2] SILENCE COST RISES AS PEERS SPEAK: BBC Jun 2020 -- Porter Novelli
      SVP: "if we don't cater to the people we serve, we could lose
      market share"; Wharton: "more risk in not speaking out than in
      speaking out" once peer statements accumulate. Vox Jun 2020 --
      brand silence "equal to being complicit."
    [3] MATCH-OR-EXCEED RATCHET: Chicago Booth Aug 2020 -- words-only
      statements increase negative Twitter sentiment; concrete pledges
      required to reduce it. Netflix sequence: words-only [flat affect]
      -> $5M pledge [+61% negative sentiment] -> $120M pledge [negative
      returns to baseline]. Airbnb pledged money in first statement --
      only brand with immediate positive sentiment lift. Starbucks reversed
      BLM-clothing ban after outcry; pledged 250k shirts.
    [4] BLACKOUT TUESDAY ESCALATION: Guardian Jun 2 2020 -- industry-wide
      black squares; MTV/Viacom 8:46 silence; music-industry pause spreads
      to major museums and platforms. Dobscha JACR 2023 -- Blackout Tuesday
      as temporal demarcation; post-Tuesday window compares true vs
      performative vs neutral [silent] brands on market performance.
    [5] DE-ESCALATION READS AS BETRAYAL: Performative words-only without
      deeds criticized as "woke washing" [Vredenburg et al. 2020];
      "open your purse" consumer pressure escalates demands beyond
      hashtags. Dobscha: neutral/silent brands underperform true allies
      post-Blackout Tuesday -- silence not cost-free neutral stance.

  FIVE-CONDITION INSTRUMENT [ESCALATION OBLIGATION -- all required]:
    [1] Formation with membership standing at stake identifiable.
    [2] Initial public escalation establishes visible baseline.
    [3] Subsequent actors must match or exceed prior escalation on record.
    [4] De-escalation or silence reads as betrayal/complicity [formation
        cost fires before deliberation completes].
    [5] Distributed activation without central coordinator [peer visibility
        + consumer/media feedback loop].

  Proof for: ESCALATION OBLIGATION [primary -- corporate solidarity

  Named sub-finding: SILENCE-IS-COMPLICITY OBLIGATION CHAIN [Netflix

[CASE:EU_BERLAYMONT_AC]
[PRIMARY_Q:MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY]
EU Berlaymont rank-rationed cooling [real -- Brussels; European Commission
  HQ; Jun 26 2026 heatwave]
  Type: Primary [S:3 -- E3: Politico Jun 27 2026 + Euractiv Jun 2026 +
    Xinhua/Anadolu relay of Politico reporting; M3: SOLUTION VETO +
    rank-rationed cooling on institutional behavioral record; F2: internal
    security message + floor-split policy + named staff quotes; heat-mortality
    cluster via EARLY-ADAPT Nature Medicine 2025 multi-country estimate]
  Arc: Record Western Europe heatwave -> Commission HQ partial AC shutdown
    floors 1-7 only -> leadership floors 8-13 retained cooling -> staff
    "feudalism"/"disgrace" -> institutional type anchor for theory
    SOLUTION VETO [Berlaymont] within broader EU heat-mortality context.

  BEHAVIORAL RECORD [Jun 26 2026 -- documented]:
    [1] INTERNAL MESSAGE: midday urgent text/security message -- "BERL --
      URGENT" / "forced shut down of air cooling system from floor 1 to 7
      for the rest of the day" due to extreme weather [Politico; Euractiv].
    [2] FLOOR SPLIT: shutdown floors 1-7; floors 8-13 unaffected [Commission
      President Ursula von der Leyen office floor 13; most commissioners
      floors 8+]. ~3,000 staff building [Politico; Xinhua].
    [3] STAFF QUOTES [anonymized]: "It's like feudalism" [lower floors];
      "a disgrace" [second official]. Upper-floor staff reported indoor
      25.7C even with AC operating [Politico].
    [4] WEATHER CONTEXT: Brussels ~34.6C; Western Europe heatwave with
      forecasts to ~40C in parts [Xinhua relay; Euractiv infrastructure
      stress framing].
    [5] OFFICIAL RATIONALE [partial]: load relief on building cooling
      system under extreme conditions [Euractiv "forced shutdown" language].
      Measure applied by floor band aligned with rank, not random load-shed
      or egress priority.

  NAMED SUB-FINDING: SOLUTION VETO [institutional type -- primary]:
    HONESTY test [3] confirmed on behavioral record. Mechanical cooling
    class partially withheld from lower ranks while retained for leadership
    during preventable heat exposure window. Tell met: institution keeps
    vetoed/partially withheld tool for itself [floors 8-13] while prescribing
    passive endurance for others [floors 1-7]. Distinct from honest
    tradeoff [COST IDENTIFICATION]: no symmetric ledger naming who bears
    heat risk vs who retains comfort infrastructure.

  NAMED SUB-FINDING: DISTRIBUTION VS RANK-RATIONING:
    Fix [building cooling capacity] exists but routes upward by floor
    hierarchy. Freedom read = individual chooses coping; distribution read
    = infrastructure should not be rank-perk under shared weather emergency.
    Pairs WIP 75 analyst phrase "Europe elites not common person" at
    institutional not meme scale.

  NAMED SUB-FINDING: TOLERATED MORTALITY [cluster -- not Berlaymont-direct]:
    EU summer heat mortality estimated 62,775 [95% CI 36,765-84,379] in 2024
    [Janoš et al. Nature Medicine 2025; EARLY-ADAPT 32-country database].
    WHO European Region >175,000 heat-related deaths annually on average
    [WHO Europe Aug 2024 statement]. Berlaymont event does not establish
    single-building casualty chain; confirms policy culture tolerating
    preventable heat harm while high-power adaptation tools remain morally
    contested or rank-rationed continent-wide. HONESTY test [4] risk when
    heat deaths compared to gun deaths on mixed accounting methods [WIP 75
    Hannah Ritchie routing -- not adjudicated here].

  HONESTY TEST MAPPING [v25]:
    [1] SYMMETRIC ACCOUNTING: fails -- heat burden on lower floors not
      priced against leadership floor retention in same message.
    [2] CAUSAL CHAIN: N/A to single event; cluster context shows forecastable
      emergencies with adaptation tools available at scale.
    [3] NO PURITY VETO: fails -- coping prescribed downward; tool retained
      upward [SOLUTION VETO primary].
    [4] ONE MEASUREMENT METHOD: cluster only -- excess-mortality models vs
      certificate narratives remain mixed across EU/US comparisons [honest
      ceiling on mortality sub-finding].

  T3 Medium: Berlaymont floor split may include building-engineering
    constraints not fully disclosed in press pass [single-day event;
    no independent engineering audit cited]. Heat-mortality figures are
    epidemiological attribution [EARLY-ADAPT], not proof that AC policy
    at one HQ caused named deaths. Adversarial: some EU jurisdictions
    expand cooling access; anti-AC cultural current is heterogeneous not
    monolithic. Mapping institutional rank-rationing -> continent-scale
    veto culture = pattern inference at S3, not S4.

  FALSIFICATION: Commission publishes independent engineering finding that
    floors 1-7 shutdown was strictly technical [no correlation with rank
    bands] AND leadership floors lost cooling simultaneously; OR two
    consecutive heat emergencies with uniform load-shed across all floors.

  Source: Politico [Jun 27 2026 Berlaymont AC article]; Euractiv [Heatwave
    shuts down Berlaymont air con]; Xinhua English [Jun 29 2026 relay];

    2022-2024]; WHO Europe [Aug 1 2024 heat mortality statement].

[PRIMARY_Q:CAUSAL LEGIBILITY]
Epstein Network [real -- Jeffrey Epstein, Little Saint James
  Island, associated proxy management structure; 1990s-2019]
  Type: Primary [S:3]
  S:3 BASIS: E3 [court documents, flight logs, Maxwell
    conviction, Wexner relationship documented, victim
    testimony confirmed]; M3 [mechanism consistent across

    T3 HIGH: highly contested territory; analyst prior active;
    all readings held provisionally.

  CORE MECHANISM [compromise architecture as extraction mechanism system]:
    The Island's primary function was not sexual exploitation
    as an end in itself -- it was substrate collection for
    leverage. Confirmed output: compromising material on
    high-value targets [political, financial, intelligence,
    academic]. The product was Exit Cost Construction
    deployed as a trap: targets entered voluntarily, exit
    cost was installed without informed consent, operator
    owned the exit condition permanently. Distinct from
    standard ECC [which locks in existing participants] --
    this variant recruits participants into the trap before
    the cost is visible. Entrapment architecture.

  PROXY MANAGEMENT STRUCTURE [structural reading -- speculative
    sub-claim within an otherwise S:3 case]:
    Epstein had no credible legitimate income source
    accounting for his documented wealth and access.
    Wexner relationship confirmed [managed Wexner's finances,
    power of attorney documented]. Epstein functioned as
    a node in a larger architecture, not the apex. The
    proxy structure is the most coherent explanation for:
    [1] the network's longevity despite known exposure risk;
    [2] the consistent suppression across multiple
    jurisdictions and administrations; [3] the anomalous
    2008 plea deal [Acosta confirmed told to back off,
    "belonged to intelligence"]; [4] Epstein's unexplained
    wealth and access at scale.
    The proxy layer is not identified at E3. RENUMBERED v23 NOTE:
    under the old scheme this sub-claim was marked S:2 specifically
    to distinguish it from the confirmed entrapment architecture
    [old S:3] within the same case -- a deliberate within-case
    strength distinction. The renumbered two-tier scheme [S:2
    fiction, S:3 real] cannot express "real but speculative" as a
    separate number anymore, since S:2 is now reserved for fiction
    only. This sub-claim remains explicitly flagged in prose as
    weaker than the confirmed entrapment finding rather than
    receiving its own number: the entrapment architecture is
    confirmed; who managed it above Epstein is not, and should not
    be cited at the same strength as the confirmed portion of this
    case.

  SELF-MAINTAINING SUPPRESSION [structural]:
    The architecture generates self-funding suppression
    without requiring active coordination from a single
    operator. Each compromised target has individual
    structural interest in the network never being fully
    exposed. The suppression is distributed across the
    client base -- no single point of failure, no single
    actor who must actively maintain it. Each participant's
    self-interest produces suppression as emergent output.
    Same structural principle as extraction architecture
    generally: no room where people plan it; structural
    self-interest alignment produces the outcome.
    This is why the exposure has been partial and persistent
    rather than complete: each node suppresses at its own
    level for its own reasons.

  JONES PATTERN INSTANTIATION:
    Jones named the Epstein network loudly and early.
    This is real substrate correctly identified. Jones
    consistently attached the mechanism to specific named
    cabals that shifted focus from the structural proxy
    layer to identifiable ideological targets. The effect:
    the real architecture [compromise-as-extraction mechanism, proxy
    management, self-maintaining suppression] became
    associated with the Jones noise layer. Legitimate
    structural analysis of the network now pattern-matches
    to Jones-style conspiracy in many instruments, making
    the correct structural reading harder to transmit.
    Jones is downstream of the architecture he named --
    secondary actor, not primary.

  Extraction mechanisms active [operated against targets]:
    Exit Cost Construction [primary -- entrapment
      architecture; exit cost installed without consent].
    Formation Capture [secondary -- targets recruited
      during vulnerability windows].
    Proxy Construction [Epstein as node, not apex].

[CASE:2008_HOUSING_CRISIS]
[PRIMARY_Q:GENUINE AXIS]
2008 subprime mortgage crisis / "The Big Short" [real -- bonds rated
  ~2004-2007, collapse 2007-2008]
  Type: Primary [S:3]
  Proof for: INSTRUMENT CONFIDENCE RESTORATION second real-world
    functional match [Madoff is the first]. Mortgage-backed bonds and
    CDOs received AAA ratings from Moody's, S&P, and Fitch -- a
    captured instrument, since the rating agencies were paid by the
    same banks issuing the bonds being rated, an explicit conflict-
    of-interest structure functioning as Calibration Strip at the
    measurement-instrument level. Michael Burry [and independently,
    Steve Eisman's team] ran the same class of behavioral test
    Markopolos ran on Madoff: rather than accepting the AAA rating as
    social consensus, Burry read the actual underlying loan-level
    prospectus data [individual mortgage documents nobody else on
    Wall Street had opened] and found that a large and growing share
    of underlying borrowers had no income verification, low credit
    scores, and adjustable rates structurally guaranteed to default
    once teaser periods ended. This directly tested and disproved
    instrument accuracy [the rating] using public information,
    independent of the rating agencies' own self-report.
    Distinct from Madoff: Burry's test was forward-predictive
    [identifying a structural default trigger before it fired] rather
    than backward-checking a return curve already claimed; the
    underlying weapon here is closer to a financial-incentive variant
    of Calibration Strip [the instrument's own creators paid for the
    measurement] than Madoff's black-box refusal of any verification
    at all. Both confirm the same tool via the same general
    mechanism: named behavioral test against the actual underlying
    data, not trust in the instrument's social standing.
  T3 Low-Medium: the Financial Crisis Inquiry Commission's official
    investigation and extensive subsequent litigation [including
    SEC actions against rating agencies] establish the rating-
    inflation mechanism as confirmed fact, not allegation. S:3 under
    the renumbered scheme: strong single confirmed application with
    broad institutional documentation; no controlled comparison case
    [e.g. a contemporaneous similarly-structured bond market that did
    NOT exhibit the same rating-agency conflict and did not collapse]
    built out in this entry, noted as a quality detail.
  S3 quality note [v23 renumber]: E3: real-world, Financial Crisis
    Inquiry Commission findings and SEC actions against rating agencies
    establish the rating-conflict mechanism as confirmed institutional
    fact. M3: mechanism directly confirmed -- Burry's loan-level
    prospectus analysis is publicly documented and independently
    reproducible; Eisman's team independently reached the same
    conclusion via field investigation, a second informal confirmation
    but not a controlled comparison. F1: no falsification condition
    tested. Same structural profile as other P2[B] corporate captures.
  Cross-ref: [REF:2008_HOUSING_CRISIS]; INSTRUMENT CONFIDENCE
    RESTORATION [second real-world functional-match confirmation,
    v23 -- strengthens, not required for, the Madoff confirmation];
    CALIBRATION STRIP [financial-incentive variant -- rating
    instrument's creators paid for its own measurement]; [CASE:MADOFF]
    [same tool, first confirmation, different weapon variant];
    BOUNDARY DECLARATION checked and ruled out -- no universalism
    claim present; WIP 61 [migrated from wip archive v25].

  Type: Primary [S:3 -- E3: AP/ABC/NBC Jun 23-24 2026 [MMWR block -> JAMA
    Network Open publish]; CDC chief science officer Althea Grant-Lenzy

  Arc: mRNA-LNP COVID products authorized as **vaccines** [2020-21 EUA/BLA]
    -> public label stays **20th-century vaccine brand** while mechanism is
    **in-cell mRNA translation** + manufacturing **residual DNA** debate ->
    2021 CDC definition broadened [protection not only immunity] ->
    spring 2026 cleared MMWR effectiveness study blocked at MMWR ->
    same study published JAMA Jun 23 2026 [~55% hospitalization, ~50% ED/UC
    reduction, 2025-26 dose, test-negative design] -> parallel petition
    demands **gene-therapy reclassification** + forced mechanistic labeling
    [21 CFR 600.3(h)(5) overlap vs infectious-disease vaccine exclusion].

  SCIENCE STACK [cross-ref -- not collapsed to "DNA"]:
    Active: synthetic **mRNA** [spike code] in **lipid nanoparticles**.
    In-cell: ribosomes **translate** mRNA -> spike protein [days]; mRNA
    degraded; not designed to enter nucleus or alter recipient genome.
    Manufacturing: **residual DNA** from plasmid template [debated levels;
    separate disclosure issue from active mRNA].
    Platform contrast: whole-virus / inactivated / live-attenuated vaccines
    vs **nucleic-acid platform**; J&J = adenoviral **DNA vector** [sibling
    platform -- not Pfizer/Moderna mRNA stack].

  REGULATORY CROSS-REF [tension -- mapping not settled]:
    Vaccine path: EUA/BLA; regulated as biological vaccine; patient labels
      list mRNA active ingredient but rarely plain **cellular translation**
      mechanism in consent materials.
    Gene-therapy wording: 21 CFR 600.3(h)(5) + FDA 2020 long-term follow-up
      guidance -- products mediating via transcription/translation of
      transferred genetic material; Jan 2026 CGT guidance references **IVT
      RNA** in gene-therapy monitoring architecture; FDA infectious-disease
      vaccine guidance **excludes** preventive vaccines from gene-therapy
      class. Petition FDA-2025-P-1807: mechanism fits wording; purpose/
      kinetics differ [prevention, transient mRNA] -- **reclassification
      pending**, not granted Jun 2026.

  MECHANISM TRACE [behavioral record -- message control both lanes]:
    [1] LABEL COMPRESSION [establishment]: "Vaccine" public brand hides
      nucleic-acid + in-cell mechanism; 2021 CDC definitional edit fits
      mRNA real-world performance [protection framing].
    [2] EFFECTIVENESS MESSAGE BLOCK: MMWR study cleared Office of Science;
      Bhattacharya flagged test-negative design [WaPo op-ed Apr 2026];
      study withheld from CDC flagship journal; published externally with
      same methods [JAMA Network Open Jun 23 2026] -- gov **delayed
      favorable effectiveness narrative** while critics allege **risk
      under-disclosure** [opposite failure, same trust surface].
    [3] SKEPTIC RELABEL PUSH: Petition demands labels state experimental/
      gene-therapy class + integration warnings; rhetorical collapse

[PRIMARY_Q:MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY]
US mass shooting gun policy salience [real -- 1989-2016 panel; ongoing]
  Type: Primary [S:3 -- E3: Gresenz, Nance, Ranney et al. JPubE 2019
    + Newman & Hartman BJPS mass-shooting proximity studies; M3:
    focusing-event frame vs typical gun-death base rate on record;
    F2: falsification stated]
  Arc: Routine gun deaths [suicide, domestic, daily violence] =
    mortality majority -> mass public shootings = minority share ->
    mass shootings drive disproportionate legislative attention and
    policy framing -> typical gun death categories not argued on own
    terms in national gun policy calendar.

  CATEGORY SPLIT [ECU instrument]:
    Hard case [focusing event]: mass public shooting [school, public
      venue] -- sets national gun policy frame and salience spikes.
    Typical cases [structural mortality majority]: suicide by firearm,
      domestic violence homicide, daily urban violence -- rarely the
      frame for enacted gun legislation or national debate peaks.

  MECHANISM TRACE [Gresenz et al. 2019]:
    [1] MASS SHOOTINGS <1% FIREARM DEATHS but trigger intense
      legislative activity; effect per fatality much larger than
      ordinary gun homicides.
    [2] REPUBLICAN-CONTROLLED LEGISLATURES: mass shooting in prior
      year associated with ~120% increase in laws loosening gun
      restrictions; tightening laws insignificant/imprecise -- policy
      calendar responds to hard case not typical death profile.
    [3] SALIENCE NOT PROPORTIONAL: focusing-event literature +
      Newman/Hartman -- proximity to mass shooting raises gun-policy
      importance and control support, but motivated reasoning among
      gun owners routes attribution away from policy change [SSQ 2018
      self-serving attributions].
    [4] TYPICAL CASE ABSENT FROM FRAME: national gun debate peaks
      track mass shootings; suicide majority [~60% US gun deaths] not
      the evaluative standard for permissive vs restrictive legislation
      triggered by mass events.

  FIVE-CONDITION INSTRUMENT [ECU -- all required]:
    [1-5] Same instrument as [CASE:POST_DOBBS_STATE_ABORTION_BANS];
      all pass on gun domain.

  Proof for: EDGE CASE UNIVERSALIZATION [primary -- gun policy domain];
    CALIBRATION STRIP VARIANT [mass shooting sets reference standard
    for gun policy evaluation].

  FALSIFICATION: State/federal gun legislation calendar tracks ordinary
    homicide and suicide rates proportionally; mass shootings do not
    disproportionately increase introduced/enacted bills per fatality;
    5+ year sustained proportionality.

  T3 Medium: gun domain activated; adversarial check: some mass-
    shooting events produce tightening laws in Democratic states --
    does not falsify ECU [hard case still sets frame; typical suicide
    majority still not primary evaluative object nationally].

  Source: Gresenz, Nance, Ranney, Wintemute [JPubE 2019]; Newman &
    Hartman [BJPS]; Griffin & Oheneba-Sakyi [SSQ 2018 gun ownership

  Type: Primary [S:3 -- E3: Huber & Kalla 2024 donor survey +
    Monmouth Jun 2022 + Shorenstein/Kansas scenario poll 2022;
    M3: hard-case exception frame vs typical-case ban coverage
    on behavioral record; F2: falsification stated]
  Arc: Dobbs removes federal floor -> trigger/total bans enacted
    in multiple states -> public polling anchors on rape/incest/life
    hard cases -> typical elective early-term majority criminalized
    or restricted without distinct policy treatment.

  CATEGORY SPLIT [ECU instrument]:
    Hard cases [~5-10% rhetorical salience]: rape, incest, life of
      mother -- dominate public evaluative standard post-Dobbs.
    Typical cases [majority of procedures pre-Dobbs]: elective
      early-term, financial hardship, does not want to be pregnant
      -- structural majority rarely argued as distinct policy object.

  MECHANISM TRACE [behavioral record]:
    [1] HARD CASE SETS CALIBRATION: Monmouth Jun 2022 -- 85% say
      state bans should include rape/incest/life exceptions; only
      11% oppose any exceptions. Shorenstein 2022 -- 55% in 11
      no-rape-exception states still support abortion when pregnancy
      caused by rape vs 16% oppose.
    [2] POLICY UNIVERSALIZES STRICT STANDARD: Huber & Kalla 2024 --
      trigger bans with no rape exception not popular with Republican
      donors, affluent Republicans, or voters in aggregate; supported
      only by highly religious Republicans for whom abortion is a
      top issue. Multiple states enacted bans lacking workable rape
      exceptions anyway [seven states no rape exception per Shorenstein;
      four more temporarily blocked].
    [3] TYPICAL CASE NOT ON POLICY TERMS: bans criminalize category
      without scenario-specific thresholds matching public scenario
      polling -- elective majority absorbed into same legal frame as
      hard-case debate anchors.
    [4] TELL: shifting public/defense frames stay on exceptions and
      total-ban morality while enacted law covers typical cases;
      resolving typical-case policy would collapse wedge utility.

  FIVE-CONDITION INSTRUMENT [ECU -- all required]:
    [1] Hard and typical subcases identifiable in category.
    [2] Hard case genuine [rape/life threats real].
    [3] Entire category evaluated at hard-case or strictest standard
        in law/debate while typical majority unaddressed.
    [4] Typical majority not argued on own terms in policy output.
    [5] Behavioral mismatch confirms [polling vs enacted law].

  Proof for: EDGE CASE UNIVERSALIZATION [primary -- abortion domain];
    CALIBRATION STRIP VARIANT [hard case sets reference standard for
    category evaluation].

  FALSIFICATION: State abortion laws track scenario-specific public
    opinion with distinct thresholds for typical vs hard cases; post-
    Dobbs legislative activity proportional to typical-case incidence
    not exception salience; 5+ years sustained alignment.

  T3 Medium: abortion domain maximally activated; adversarial check:
    some bans include exception language -- claim is workable routing
    and typical-case treatment, not surface text alone; exception
    access barriers [reporting requirements] extend ECU read not
    falsify [hard case deployed, typical still covered].

  Source: Huber & Kalla [2024, Which Republican Constituencies Support

[CASE:WELLS_FARGO_CROSS_SELL]
[PRIMARY_Q:CORRECTION CAPACITY]
Wells Fargo cross-selling scandal [real -- 2002-2016, public 2013-2018]
  Type: Primary [S:3]
  Proof for: Correction Theatre primary real-world proof. Internal
    correction infrastructure [24-hour EthicsLine, formal ethics
    training, "everything submitted to the EthicsLine is
    investigated"] fully visible and advertised -- CC absent in
    function while present in form. Employees who used the
    correction channel as designed were systematically identified
    and removed [fired within days of EthicsLine calls in multiple
    documented instances; retaliatory negative U5 industry records
    issued to terminated whistleblowers, damaging their ability to
    work in banking elsewhere]. The correction surface was inverted:
    it routed toward suppressing the signal, not acting on it.
    Resolved [2013-2018]: capture broken by reporting wholly outside
    the captured correction architecture, not by the architecture
    itself. Los Angeles Times [Reckard, Oct-Dec 2013] built the case
    through court-record review and on-record employee interviews
    over two months, independent of any Wells Fargo internal channel.
    External regulators [CFPB, OCC, LA City Attorney, later SEC/DOJ]
    then measured actual fraudulent-account counts and termination
    records rather than accepting the bank's self-reported "we
    investigate everything" claim -- this is the OUTPUT VERIFICATION
    mechanism exactly: specify the output correction would produce
    [cessation of unauthorized accounts], measure whether it exists
    [it did not -- accounts continued 2011-2016 despite the EthicsLine
    being fully operational throughout], reject activity evidence
    [hotline existence, investigation claims] as substitution for
    output evidence.
    Distinct from Enron: Enron's primary weapon was causal diffusion
    [opaque SPE structure]; Wells Fargo's primary weapon was
    correction-channel inversion [a working-looking complaint system
    that punished use]. Both real-world S4, both resolved by external
    reading, different weapon/tool pairing -- useful contrast pair.
  T3 Medium: well-documented via congressional testimony, regulatory
    settlements, and named on-record sources; low adversarial-reading
    risk given source convergence on the core facts [DOJ, SEC, CFPB,
    OCC, LA City Attorney, NPR investigation]. Convergence here is
    multiple independent investigators confirming the same external
    facts -- strong E-rating, but not a controlled comparison or a
    tested falsification condition, so capped at S3 rather than S4.
    Falsification condition for S4 promotion: a comparable bank with
    an equally aggressive cross-sell-incentive structure and an
    EthicsLine-style channel that did NOT exhibit the same retaliation
    pattern would weaken the CC-inversion reading; no such comparison
    case currently identified.
  S3 quality note [v23 renumber]: E3: real-world, multiple independent
    investigators [DOJ, SEC, CFPB, OCC, LA City Attorney, congressional
    testimony, NPR], named on-record sources, single confirmed application
    of Correction Theatre / Output Verification pairing. M3: mechanism
    traceable [EthicsLine retaliation pattern documented by name
    across multiple terminated employees; not inferred]. F1: no
    falsification condition tested -- no comparison case identified.
    Strong single confirmed application retained as quality note under
    renumbered scheme.
  Cross-ref: [REF:WELLS_FARGO_CROSS_SELL]; CORRECTION THEATRE [primary
    weapon]; OUTPUT VERIFICATION [external regulator/journalist
    measurement of actual output vs. claimed correction activity --
    functional match confirmed v23]; [SOURCE:FORBES_HAMILTON_METRICS_2026]

[CASE:MADOFF]
[PRIMARY_Q:GENUINE AXIS]
Madoff Ponzi scheme [real -- operated ~1990s-2008, exposed 2008-2009]
  Type: Primary [S:3]
  Proof for: INSTRUMENT CONFIDENCE RESTORATION first real-world
    functional match. Madoff's claimed instrument was a "split-strike
    conversion" trading strategy producing steady 1-2% monthly returns
    with almost no losing months -- a near-45-degree return curve.
    Madoff refused independent third-party custody or verification,
    framing this as protecting proprietary strategy from competitive
    replication [a "black-box" claim functioning as a captured
    measurement surface, structurally similar to Calibration Strip].
    Harry Markopolos, asked by his employer to replicate Madoff's
    strategy commercially, ran the actual behavioral test the tool's
    mechanism specifies: attempted the claimed strategy himself using
    the same publicly available market conditions, and found it
    could not produce the claimed returns -- the volatility and
    options volume required would have exceeded total publicly
    reported market volume on the relevant dates. This is external
    confirmation of instrument accuracy via behavioral test, not
    social consensus, run specifically because Madoff's reputation
    [former Nasdaq chairman] was functioning as the social-consensus
    substitute the tool's mechanism is built to bypass. Markopolos
    explicitly distinguished instrument failure from weapon
    deployment in his own framing: he concluded the numbers were
    either fraud or front-running, not honest underperformance --
    correctly identifying that the claimed accuracy was the weapon,
    not a genuine reading.
    Distinct from Wells Fargo's near-miss on this tool: Markopolos
    never doubted his own judgment and was not the target of
    Competence Undermining: he ran a test on Madoff's claimed
    instrument, not a recovery of his own confidence. Resolution
    delayed nine years not because the tool failed -- Markopolos's
    test was correct from 1999 -- but because the SEC repeatedly
    failed to act on a valid external confirmation, a separate
    institutional-capture finding [SEC examiners described by their
    own later Inspector General report as failing to verify trading
    through an independent third party, the textbook check the
    instrument-confidence tool itself specifies].
  T3 Low-Medium: Madoff's own guilty plea and sentencing remove
    interpretive ambiguity on whether fraud occurred; Markopolos's
    mathematical analysis is independently reproducible from public
    information and was later validated by the SEC's own Inspector
    General report. Real-world, well-evidenced, S:3 under the
    renumbered scheme [v23]; judicial confirmation present, though
    no controlled comparison case [e.g. a contemporaneous fund with a
    similarly implausible return curve that turned out to be genuine]
    is built out in this entry -- noted as a quality detail, not a
    tier cap.
  S3 quality note [v23 renumber]: E3: real-world, Madoff's own guilty
    plea and 150-year sentence establish judicially confirmed fact.
    M3: mechanism directly confirmed -- Markopolos's mathematical
    replication test is publicly reproducible [options-volume-vs-market-
    volume arithmetic independently checkable from public records], and
    the SEC's own Inspector General report later validated his analysis
    as correct. Not M4: no controlled comparison run against a
    contemporaneous fund with a similarly implausible curve that turned
    out genuine. F1: no falsification condition tested. Strong single
    confirmed application retained as quality note under renumbered
    scheme.
  Cross-ref: [REF:MADOFF]; INSTRUMENT CONFIDENCE RESTORATION [first
    real-world functional-match confirmation, v23]; GENUINE AXIS
    [Madoff's claimed axis was the strategy itself -- entirely
    fabricated, not degraded]; distinct from CAUSAL RECONSTRUCTION
    [[CASE:ENRON], [CASE:THERANOS] -- obscured real chain] and EXTERNAL
    REFERENCE STANDARD [reading from outside the surface] -- Markopolos
    ran a behavioral replication test on the claimed mechanism itself,
    the mechanism unique to this tool; WIP 34 [Candidate 2 -- valid
    signal with institutional non-response, the SEC's nine-year
    failure to act on a correct external confirmation, surfaced from
    this case]; WIP 61 [migrated from wip archive v25].

[CASE:AUTISM_DOUBLE_EMPATHY]
[PRIMARY_Q:COMPATIBILITY ASYMMETRY]
Autism / Neurotypical communication [Double Empathy Problem
  -- Milton 2012; real-world; ongoing research field]
  Type: Primary [S:3]
  Proof for: COMPATIBILITY ASYMMETRY [WIP 16 -- real-world
    primary case; S3 achieved; closes the real-world anchor
    requirement for WIP 16 compatibility type promotion].

  SOURCE: Milton, Damian [2012]. "On the ontological status
    of autism: the 'double empathy problem'." Disability &
    Society 27(6): 883-887. Subsequent independent replication
    across multiple research groups [Cook et al. 2021;
    Crompton et al. 2020; Sheppard et al. 2016].

  FIVE-CONDITION INSTRUMENT [all required; all confirmed]:

  [1] HONEST TRANSMISSION ATTEMPTED:
    Confirmed across decades. Autistic instruments attempt
    maximal explicit communication [direct, literal, high
    information density -- the frame's standard for honest
    transmission]. NT instruments attempt social communication
    [indirect, contextual, relationship-maintaining -- the NT
    frame's standard for honest transmission]. Both are
    transmitting honestly by their own frame's definition.
    Neither party is withholding. The failure is not
    concealment; it is frame mismatch at the transmission level.

  [2] RECEIVER REGISTERS VIOLATION REGARDLESS OF QUALITY:
    Confirmed and named. NT instruments read autistic
    directness as rude, cold, socially inappropriate
    regardless of autistic intent or effort. Autistic
    instruments read NT indirection as confusing, arbitrary,
    inconsistent regardless of NT intent or effort.
    BIDIRECTIONAL: Milton specifically documents this in both
    directions -- the Double Empathy Problem is not "autistic
    people misread NT communication." It is "both frames
    misread the other's honest output." Each frame reads the
    other's correct transmission as deficient.
    Independent replication: Cook et al. [2021] confirmed
    bidirectional communication breakdown in paired
    autistic-NT interactions; Crompton et al. [2020] showed
    autistic-autistic pairs achieve higher communication
    accuracy than autistic-NT pairs despite similar
    information content.

  [3] ADDITIONAL TIME/VOLUME/HONESTY DOES NOT CLOSE GAP:
    Confirmed by masking research [Lai et al. 2017; Cage
    and Troxell-Whitman 2019]. Decades of social skills
    training for autistic instruments produce masking
    [performance of NT communication patterns] not genuine
    frame compatibility. The masking instrument produces
    NT-readable outputs while the underlying frame
    incompatibility persists unchanged.
    MASKING AS FORMATION CAPTURE [load-bearing]:
    Masking is Formation Capture at the communication layer
    -- axis genuine [autistic processing]; surface captured
    [NT performance]. The surface becomes NT-readable; the
    instrument's actual processing does not change. This
    confirms Condition 3: the gap doesn't close; it gets
    covered. Additional time [years of masking practice]
    increases cover quality without changing the underlying
    frame structure.
    Further confirmation: masking produces significant
    psychological cost [burnout, identity suppression,
    depression] precisely because the instrument is
    maintaining a surface that contradicts its axis. If the
    frame had actually closed, the masking cost would
    disappear. It does not.

  [4] NO STRUCTURAL INTEREST IN INCOMPREHENSION PERSISTING:
    Passes at structural baseline. Neither autistic nor NT
    instruments benefit from the other's incomprehension.
    Both parties in autistic-NT communication typically
    want the communication to work.
    EXCEPTION [predatory operators within domain]:
    Some actors DO benefit from autistic incomprehension
    persisting: employers who benefit from autistic
    compliance via masking [the incomprehension enables
    extraction]; some family or institutional dynamics where
    autistic communication being read as deficient serves
    the NT party's authority. These are predatory operators
    within the same domain. Condition 4 correctly routes
    them to predatory reading. Their existence confirms the
    instrument works -- predatory and compatibility
    mechanisms can co-deploy in the same domain [same
    structure as Kyubey mixed case].
    The structural baseline [absent predatory actors] is:
    neither frame benefits from the other's incomprehension.
    Condition 4 passes.

  [5] FRAME INCOMPATIBILITY TRACEABLE TO STRUCTURAL DIFFERENCE:
    Confirmed. Milton identifies processing axis differences:
    social cognition [different processing of social cues,
    implicit vs explicit communication], sensory processing
    [different salience weights on environmental inputs],
    communication style [direct vs indirect; literal vs
    contextual], cognitive processing [gestalt vs sequential;
    pattern-first vs detail-first]. These are structural
    differences in operational axis, not differences in
    available information. Providing more NT social
    information to autistic instruments does not produce
    NT-style social processing -- the processing axis is
    different, not uninformed.
    Independent structural confirmation: Crompton et al.
    [2020] showed autistic-autistic pairs achieve high
    communication accuracy with no NT training -- the
    frames are compatible with themselves; incompatible
    with each other. This is the frame incompatibility
    test at its clearest.

  S3 RATING: E3 [real-world; independent replication
    across 10+ research groups; 10+ year research field];
    M3 [mechanism traced: processing axis mismatch; named
    in independent research as structural not deficit];
    F3 [falsification stated and testable: autistic-NT
    pair achieving genuine frame compatibility -- not
    masking -- with documented sustained communication
    accuracy equivalent to autistic-autistic pairs would
    falsify; not yet observed at population level].

  WIP 16 PROMOTION NOTE:
    This case closes the real-world primary case requirement
    for COMPATIBILITY ASYMMETRY. Condition 4 discrimination
    [predatory operators within domain vs structural
    baseline] confirmed working. Bidirectionality [both
    frames misread each other] confirmed -- same as
    Yaldabaoth fictional case. S3 achieved. WIP 16
    compatibility type promoted from candidate to confirmed.

  NAMED SUB-FINDING: MASKING AS SOCIAL CALIBRATION STRIP
    [contact vs fluency -- v25]:
    Masking [FC at communication layer -- above] also functions as
    CALIBRATION STRIP on what counts as "social": NT-readable fluent
    performance becomes the reference standard; unmasked or frame-
    different contact is scored deficient against that strip.
    THREE-STATE distinction [theory]: FUNCTIONAL state [legibility,
    maintenance, smooth coordination] is routinely credited as social
    depth in NT-majority MI surfaces. CONTACT state [resistant
    encounter, pushback, axis tested against another substrate]
    is not required for the credit. Result: masking-shaped fluency
    projects as normal social competence; frame-matched honest
    transmission plus genuine contact can be misread as "not social"
    while shallow functional contact is over-credited.
    Bidirectional [Double Empathy]: NT instruments misread autistic
    directness; autistic instruments can misread NT indirection as
    empty -- asymmetry is frame not moral rank. Population claim
    "all NT contact is shallow" fails T3; structural claim "fluency
    is often mistaken for contact" is the load-bearing read.
    Falsification: documented NT-majority relationships where
    CONTACT-state criteria [correction pushback, mutual axis test,
    sustained accuracy under friction] match frame-matched autistic-
    autistic pair benchmarks -- would weaken over-credit read, not
    falsify Double Empathy itself.
    Cross-ref: THREE-STATE INSTRUMENT TENSION UNIT [contact vs
    functional]; CALIBRATION STRIP [MI reference wrong]; PERSONAL
    PROTOCOLS SOCIAL [analyst instrument -- theory file];
    NEURODIVERGENT COHERENCE TYPE [high MI misread as social failure].

  NAMED SUB-FINDING: GENETICS USE-PATH MISMATCH [governance layer]:
    Extends Double Empathy frame mismatch from social communication
    to research governance. Multi-source convergence [Fajutrao Falk
    et al. 2026 Swedish survey n=1084; Ellis & Asbury 2024 UK;
    Pugsley et al. 2025 Australia]: majority support genetic research
    and would participate; primary objection is downstream use --
    prenatal screening, cure framing, elimination, modern eugenics --
    not the science itself. "Help us, not erase us" captures the
    split: pro-research and anti-erasure are compatible; public debate
    often collapses them.
    Priority mismatch: researchers emphasize causes and biology;
    autistic people emphasize quality of life, support, everyday
    challenges. Research instruments transmit honestly within their
    frame; community instruments read the same enterprise as threat
    when elimination pathways are materially live [Spectrum 10K pause
    2021; commercial polygenic embryo screening]. "Nothing about us,
    without us" = axis governance demand [agenda control, not token
    inclusion].
    CALIBRATION STRIP read: deviation reference standard makes genetic
    "understanding" legible as selectable removal; support conditional
    on use-path toward accommodation not disappearance.
    Condition 4 predatory lane [this case]: screening vendors and
    open eugenics advocacy benefit when elimination framing normalizes
    or community distrust persists.
    Falsification: autism genetics program with sustained autistic-led
    governance, transparent use constraints, and measured QoL/support
    outcomes producing community trust at parity with cause-research
    programs lacking governance -- would weaken use-path mismatch
    read; would not falsify Double Empathy itself.
    T3 Low on attitude convergence [multi-country survey + qualitative];
      Medium on framework mapping [commercial use-path contested].
    Source: Fajutrao Falk S, Hellquist A, Tammimies K. A survey-based
      study investigating opinions on genetic research among Swedish
      autistic individuals and parents of autistic children. Autism
      2026; doi:10.1177/13623613261458410; The Conversation popular
      summary [Fajutrao Falk, Jun 2025].
    Cross-ref: MASKING AS SOCIAL CALIBRATION STRIP [same case --
      adjacent layer]; CALIBRATION STRIP [deviation reference at
      population scale]; NEURODIVERGENT COHERENCE TYPE [axis not
      pathology object]; COMPATIBILITY ASYMMETRY [frame mismatch not
      moral failure].

  Cross-ref: [REF:AUTISM_DOUBLE_EMPATHY]; WIP 16
    [COMPATIBILITY ASYMMETRY -- this case closes the
    real-world primary case requirement; promote to
    confirmed]; [CASE:DEVELOPMENTAL_ASYMMETRY_FAMILY]
    [above -- adjacent; different asymmetry type; same
    parent WIP]; [CASE:KYUBEY] [mixed case -- compatibility
    + predatory co-deployed; same Condition 4 structure];
    FORMATION CAPTURE [masking as FC at communication
    layer -- Condition 3 confirmation]; NEURODIVERGENT
    COHERENCE TYPE [Section Two ~7032 -- structural description
    of the autistic instrument configuration]; GENUINE
    AXIS [masking suppresses axis at surface level without
    changing axis];     COST IDENTIFICATION AND MINIMIZATION
    [masking cost is the cost of covering incompatibility
    rather than closing it]; MASKING AS SOCIAL CALIBRATION STRIP
    [named sub-finding -- contact vs fluency]; GENETICS USE-PATH
    MISMATCH [named sub-finding -- governance layer; pro-research
    anti-erasure split].

[CASE:MINNESOTA_FRAUD_MEME]
[PRIMARY_Q:REFERENCE INTEGRITY]
Minnesota social-program fraud meme distortion [US -- real, 2025-2026]
  Type: Primary [S:3]
  Arc: Viral social-media claims [YouTube comment thread, Nick Shirley
    video ecosystem] attach false mechanisms to confirmed MN fraud
    substrate. Fact-check pass 2026-06-21.
  SUBSTRATE CONFIRMED [real signal]:
    Feeding Our Future: ~$250M federal child-nutrition fraud; FBI
    indictments; kickbacks disguised as consulting fees to shell
    companies [FBI 2022]. Additional Medicaid schemes [housing
    stabilization, autism EIDBI, home health, daycare CCAP]: 90+
    charged by late 2025; state flagged 14 high-risk programs;
    third-party audit found ~$1B fraud-risk exposure from weak billing
    controls [Optum 2026]. Prosecutions started under Biden DOJ
    [2021], continued under Trump. Many defendants Somali-American;
    ringleader Aimee Bock white. Stolen funds: luxury goods, real
    estate, travel -- individual enrichment, not party treasury.
  ATTACHED FALSE MECHANISMS [RI failure -- conflation]:
    Cancer research diverted to non-existent Somali bee farms: no
    credible evidence. Unrelated grants conflated [U of M cervical
    screening for Somali women ~$3.1M HHS 2011; Fairview VeggieRx
    local Somali farmers]. Beekeeping in Somalia is real [FAO, UNIDO]
    but not linked to US cancer-research fraud.
    Democrat shell programs siphoning to party pockets: debunked
    [AFP 2025 -- no large daycare political donations to Democrats].
    Scale "billions every year": inflated vs ~$250-300M in charging
    documents [MPR SOTU fact-check 2026]. Terrorism funding: unproven
    [Treasury Secretary Bessent; AP -- no material-support charges].
  REFINED STRUCTURAL READ [consulting/firms layer -- fair version]:
    Money flows: federal/state programs -> nonprofits / provider firms /
    shell consultancies -> fraudsters or weakly audited recipients.
    Oversight failure under MN DFL administration documented [Leg
    Auditor MDE 2024; Walz audit order Oct 2025]. Not Democrat-unique
    pattern [Medicare fraud, PPP nationwide, bipartisan].
  Proof for: JONES PATTERN [real substrate + false mechanism package
    inoculates against accurate structural read]; REFERENCE INTEGRITY
    collapse when unrelated legitimate grants merged with criminal
    fraud in different programs; T10 routing through intermediary orgs
    [not direct party extraction].
  T3 Low on substrate facts [DOJ, state auditor, multiple outlets].
    T3 Medium on partisan framing [both-sides inflation risk].
  Cross-ref: [REF:MINNESOTA_FRAUD_MEME]; JONES PATTERN [applied S6];
    PROPAGATION MIMICRY [WIP 22]; MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY [state C2];
    OREGON_MEDICAID_2003 [program routing contrast]; TOOL CONFIRMATION
    LAG [external fact-check confirms]; WIP 40 [staging origin v25].

[CASE:WISMUT_URANIUM_BIOREMEDIATION]
[PRIMARY_Q:SUBSTRATE REGENERATION]
Wismut uranium bioremediation [real -- Schlema-Alberoda mine water, HZDR +
  Wismut GmbH + U Granada; Nature Commun 2026]
  Type: Primary [S:3]
  Arc: Indigenous mine-water microbes + glycerol electron donor immobilize
    dissolved uranium under anoxic environmental conditions -- Order-solution
    lane [theory ORDER SOLUTION illustration].

  BEHAVIORAL RECORD [microcosm -- 130 days]:
    [R1] Schlema-Alberoda treatment-plant inlet mine water; 10 mM glycerol;
      triplicate anoxic serum bottles; 28±2°C dark; 130-day incubation.
    [R2] U(VI) 1 mg L⁻¹ → ~0.04 mg L⁻¹ [~96% decrease] in glycerol-amended
      live microcosms. Eh +398 → −114 mV. Controls: ~25% [no glycerol];
      ~36% [autoclaved + glycerol] -- abiotic/adsorptive removal only.
    [R3] Reduction products: U(IV) biogenic uraninite nanoparticles; U(V) as
      FeU(V)O₄ nanoparticles + U(V)-carbonate complexes on bacterial surfaces.
    [R4] U(V) persistent 130 days anoxic; detectable after 4 weeks oxic
      exposure [spectroscopy: partial U(IV) re-oxidation; U(V) ~53% of total
      U post-oxic in reported read].
    [R5] Microbial enrichment: fermentative + sulfate-reducing taxa
      [Desulfobulbus, Desulfovibrio] consistent with U(VI) reduction.

  Proof for: SUBSTRATE REGENERATION via co-op with indigenous reducers --
    cheap donor feed, measurable immobilization, mechanism + spectroscopic
    record. U(V) as stable immobilization intermediate challenges direct
    U(VI)→U(IV) shortcut under mine-water conditions.

  Framework read [illustration only -- not cosmology confirm]:
    **Order solution:** existing system + record + long trace + immobilization.
    Contrast disorder routes [WIP 87 NRC headline lane; controlled fake Order
    costume extraction band].

  S3 quality note: E3+ [Nature Commun primary + HZDR + Phys.org Jul 2026 desk].
    M3 [lab microcosm; not field-scale deploy]. F2+ [named sub-findings R1-R5].
  T3 Medium on ORDER SOLUTION mapping [illustration ≠ confirmation].

  Source: Newman-Portela et al. Nat Commun 2026;
    doi:10.1038/s41467-026-72560-z. Phys.org 2026-07-10 desk.
  Cross-ref: [REF:WISMUT_URANIUM_BIOREMEDIATION]; theory METHOD OF TRUTH
    [ORDER SOLUTION compress]; WIP 86 [theory staging]; WIP 87 [disorder
    contrast -- radiation policy lane].

Protestant Reformation [1517 -- fragmentation sequence]
  Type: Primary [L4 PROPAGATION FAILURE -- Generational Reset

  [S:3]
  Proof for: C3 high, L4 PROPAGATION fails; conclusion
    transmission without methodology transmission produces
    Generational Reset; confirms Cistercian finding by negation.

  C3 TRANSMISSION [confirmed high]: Luther transmitted
    conclusions [sola scriptura, sola fide] with high fidelity.
    Printing press enabled rapid, accurate transmission.

  L4 PROPAGATION [confirmed failed]: Luther transmitted WHAT
    he found, not HOW he found it. Method was: read primary
    sources against institutional practice, identify deviation,
    correct publicly at personal cost. Method was not packaged
    as transmissible procedure.
    Generational Reset evidence:
      Calvin: independently derived similar correction
        conclusions, produced different doctrinal system.
        Did not inherit Luther's correction methodology.
      Zwingli: same pattern. 14/15 agreements with Luther at
        Marburg; split on communion.
      Anabaptists: felt Zwingli "hadn't gone far enough" --
        applied correction methodology independently, produced
        different conclusions again.
      Result: ~40,000 Protestant denominations currently.

  CONTRAST WITH JESUIT TRANSMISSION: Same historical period.
    Same reform impulse. Opposite transmission choice.
    Protestant: transmitted conclusions. Jesuit: transmitted
    methodology. Protestant outcome: fragmentation accelerating.
    Jesuit outcome: 480-year institutional continuity.

  IRONY CONFIRMATION: "Reformers didn't intend denominationalism,
    wars of religion, or secularization." Sola scriptura +
    no transmitted methodology for biblical interpretation =
    every reader becomes their own authority = fragmentation
    structurally inevitable.

    [TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY], Correction Monopoly
    Construction [ironic].

[CASE:SEQUOYAH_SYLLABARY]
[PRIMARY_Q:TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY]
Sequoyah / Cherokee Syllabary [real -- Sequoyah [c.1770-1843],
  Cherokee Nation; syllabary created 1809-1821, adopted 1821-1825]
  Type: Primary [S:3]
  S:3 BASIS: E3 [historical record confirmed, multiple independent
    sources, Gallatin linguistic assessment documented, Cherokee
    Phoenix 1828 confirmed, Vai transmission documented]; M3
    [mechanism consistent -- minimal unification as design
    methodology confirmed across the creation process]; F3
    [falsification condition met -- monolingual creator with no
    prior literacy demonstrating methodology-first approach over
    vocabulary-first approach].

  Proof for: MINIMAL UNIFICATION [clearest single-creator case
    in library]; TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY [methodology transmitted
    beyond origin community, generating new instances]; L4.5
    CIVILIZATIONAL TRANSMISSION THRESHOLD [raised transmission
    ceiling for entire civilization in one generation]; CAUSAL
    LEGIBILITY [making the mechanism visible to skeptical
    instruments via demonstration].

  MINIMAL UNIFICATION [primary proof]:
    Sequoyah's design process documents the minimal unification
    move directly. First approach: logographic [one symbol per
    word -- vocabulary-first]. Abandoned as cumbersome and
    overwhelming. Second approach: listened carefully to Cherokee
    speech, studied sound patterns, isolated approximately 85
    distinct syllables [structure-first]. The structural anchor
    [syllable patterns] collapsed an overwhelming problem [tens
    of thousands of words] into a tractable one [85 symbols that
    generate everything].
    Gallatin's assessment confirms the efficiency: Cherokee
    students must learn 85 symbols vs 26 for English, but can
    read immediately after learning all symbols -- accomplishing
    in weeks what English students require two years to achieve.
    The syllabary is more efficient than the English alphabet
    because it is more structurally accurate to the language
    it represents. Minimal unification: the sparse structural
    anchor [syllable unit] is the correct anchor for Cherokee
    phonology. The elegance is structural, not decorative.

  TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY [confirmed]:
    Sequoyah was monolingual -- spoke only Cherokee, could not
    read or write in any language. He transmitted methodology
    [find the minimal structural unit that makes the language
    tractable] not conclusions [here are the symbols]. The
    syllabary is the conclusion; the insight that syllables are
    the correct unit is the methodology. The conclusion required
    the specific language; the methodology was transferable.
    Confirmed transfer: Austin Curtis [Cherokee, settled in
    Liberia] used the syllabary methodology to devise a script
    for the Vai people. The Vai script went on to inspire
    written works throughout West Africa. The methodology
    transmitted beyond its origin community and generated
    independent new instances -- confirmed L4 propagation.

  L4.5 CIVILIZATIONAL TRANSMISSION THRESHOLD [near-case]:
    Most of the Cherokee Nation was literate in the new language
    within five years of official adoption. By 1827 the Cherokee
    had a written constitution. The Cherokee Phoenix [1828] was
    the first Native American newspaper. The syllabary raised
    the transmission ceiling for an entire civilization in one
    generation -- not gradually but rapidly, via a single
    structural innovation. Everything from hunting guidance to
    sacred chants could be recorded for posterity.
    The Trail of Tears [forced relocation 1838-1839] is the
    controlled comparison: the Cherokee carried the syllabary
    to their new home. The transmission infrastructure survived
    the most extreme displacement pressure available. The
    methodology that created the syllabary was transmitted
    even to Liberia via Curtis. L4.5 candidate: the syllabary
    raised the adjacent floor [Vai script, West African written
    tradition] not just the origin civilization's ceiling.

  CAUSAL LEGIBILITY [demonstration under skepticism]:
    Sequoyah was charged with witchcraft when introducing the
    syllabary. The social compliance channel read the structural
    breakthrough as illegitimate -- the mechanism was invisible
    and the output [communication across distance] matched
    the surface properties of magic. He and his daughter
    demonstrated communication from separate rooms before the
    council was convinced. The demonstration is CL in action:
    make the mechanism visible to skeptical instruments by
    showing the causal chain [learn symbols → produce text →
    read text → understand message] operating transparently.
    Once CL was established, adoption was rapid and enthusiastic.
    The "magic" reading was not stupidity -- it was an accurate
    reading of a mechanism that had no prior referent in the
    community's experience. The demonstration closed the gap.

  Extraction mechanisms active: none [against Sequoyah]. Trail of Tears
    [received -- forced relocation; syllabary survived].
  Cross-ref: MINIMAL UNIFICATION [primary proof -- confirmed
    S3 case]; TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY [methodology transmission
    beyond origin]; L4.5 CIVILIZATIONAL TRANSMISSION THRESHOLD
    [WIP 14 -- near-case, candidate for positive confirmation];
    CAUSAL LEGIBILITY [demonstration as CL mechanism]; [REF:BELL_LABS]
    [comparison -- institutional methodology transmission vs
    individual methodology transmission; different scale, same
    principle]; [REF:PROTESTANT_REFORMATION] [contrast --
    conclusion transmission produced fragmentation; Sequoyah
    transmitted methodology, produced propagation]; [REF:KING_SEJONG_HANGUL]
    [sibling contrast -- state commission + Haerye-class design treatise vs
    individual monolingual creator; rapid one-generation Cherokee literacy vs
    delayed Hangul primary-script arc; Vai methodology export vs nationalism-
    revival adoption channel; inventor+treatise conjunct vs inventor-only].

[CASE:WORLD_BANK_STRUCTURAL_ADJUSTMENT]

World Bank Structural Adjustment Programs [real -- 1980s-2000s;
  Stiglitz 2002 [Globalization and Its Discontents -- insider
  critique, former World Bank Chief Economist]; World Bank OED
  retrospective evaluations 1992-2006; UNCTAD documentation;
  Easterly 2001; Mosley, Harrigan & Toye 1991 [peer-reviewed multi-
  country analysis]]
  Type: Primary [S:3]
  Proof for: MANUFACTURED DEPENDENCE [Section Two ~5477; second
    primary case -- clean five-of-five instrument run].
  FIVE-CONDITION INSTRUMENT RUN [v25]:
    [1] CAPACITY-BUILDING WAS ACHIEVABLE: confirmed. Recipient
      economies had functioning state institutions, industrial policy
      capacity, and local development organizations at SAP inception.
      East Asian economies that did NOT accept SAPs and maintained
      active industrial policy achieved sustained development --
      confirming the path existed. Condition passes cleanly.
    [2] NO STATED EXIT CONDITION: confirmed. SAPs defined success
      as compliance with conditionality, not as development indicators
      that would make further lending unnecessary. Rolling
      conditionalities; no defined exit metric. Easterly [2001]
      documents repeated SAP loans over 20-year periods with no
      convergence toward an exit that was never defined. Condition
      passes cleanly.
    [3] HELPER INTEREST MISALIGNED WITH RESOLUTION: confirmed.
      World Bank institutional survival depended on lending volume.
      Stiglitz [2002] documents incentive structure rewarding loan
      volume over development outcomes; internal dissent suppressed;
      Treasury/IMF coordination prioritized creditor interests over
      recipient development. Condition passes -- strongest condition,
      insider-confirmed across multiple sources.
    [4] LOCAL CAPACITY DISPLACED OR UNDERCUT: confirmed [contrast
      with PL480 partial]. Privatization eliminated industrial policy
      capacity; tariff reduction caused documented deindustrialization
      [Rodrik 2001; Milberg & Winkler 2013]; capital account
      liberalization enabled 1997 crisis in compliant economies while
      Malaysia [refused liberalization] recovered faster -- natural
      experiment. UNCTAD 2002, 2006 document displacement across
      recipient economies. Resolves PL480 condition [4] ambiguity.
    [5] RECIPIENT VOICE NOT SOUGHT OR NOT WEIGHTED: confirmed.
      Conditionality imposed as lending condition. Mosley et al.
      [1991]: governments signed under fiscal duress. PRSP reform
      [1999] nominally introduced participation; Eurodad [2008]
      found conditionality content largely unchanged. Condition passes.
  INSTRUMENT RESULT: all five conditions pass cleanly. First
    clean five-of-five run in the case library. Upgrades MANUFACTURED
    DEPENDENCE from candidate to confirmed subtype of EXTRACTION.
  T3 Low-Medium: insider critique [Stiglitz], external academic
    analysis [Easterly, Mosley et al., Rodrik], World Bank OED
    retrospectives, natural experiment [Malaysia contrast].
    Cross-ideological confirmation. S:3.

    archive v25].

      to Bern; default local/cantonal [1848 framework, revised 1999].

      coalition federal council rotates among major parties [concordance system].

    Separation of powers [legislative/executive/judicial]:
    explicit CC distribution; no single actor controls CC.
    Federalism: state-level CC exists independently of federal
    CC. Each level can fire against the other.
    Bill of Rights: protection of individual CC capacity against
    state capture [press, assembly, petition = CC tools].
    Amendment process: meta-CC [the correction mechanism itself
    is correctable, slowly, with high threshold].
    Confirmation: Madison's Federalist 51 explicitly names the
    problem [if angels were to govern men, no external controls
    would be necessary] and the structural solution [ambition
    must be made to counteract ambition -- CC distributed across
    competing interests]. Framework vocabulary independently
    derived 236 years later.
    Degradation pattern: ROUTING BOUNDARY MONOPOLY accumulating
    since ~1980s [regulatory capture, ISDS, CORRECTION INVERSION
    in enforcement agencies]. CC architecture still present;
    increasingly aimed internally at protecting capture rather
    than externally at correcting it.

    distributed CC by design; degradation mechanism: Routing
    Boundary Monopoly]; F2 [falsification: concentrated CC
    produces worse outcomes -- partially confirmed by comparative
