[CASE:KING_SEJONG_HANGUL]
[PRIMARY_Q:CAUSAL LEGIBILITY + MINIMAL UNIFICATION]
King Sejong / Hangul [real -- Joseon dynasty; Hunminjeongeum 1443; promulgated
  1446; Haerye commentary; r. Sejong 1418-1450]
  Type: Primary [S:3 -- E3: Space Daily desk primary Jun 2026 + Hunminjeongeum
    Haerye [Kansong copy; UNESCO Memory of World] + Choe Manri 1444 memorial
    in Veritable Records; M3: featural jamo design + block assembly as minimal
    unification move; Haerye "Explanation of the Design of the Letters" makes
    articulatory/cosmological rationale legible; F3: falsification stated]
  Arc: Chinese-character literacy monopoly [Hanja; ~3-5% male yangban] blocks
    Korean phonology -> Sejong + Hall of Worthies design featural alphabet ->
    elite resistance [Choe Manri 1444] -> vernacular stigma [amgeul / onmun] ->
    delayed primary-script status [Gabo 1894 permission; colonial identity
    channel; post-1945 sole script both Koreas].

  Proof for: CAUSAL LEGIBILITY [Haerye-class published design rationale on
    major national script -- strongest library pin on inventor+rationale conjunct];
    MINIMAL UNIFICATION [jamo sparse anchor + syllable-block assembly collapses
    logographic load for Korean speech]; TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY [tempered --
    design survived centuries of non-primary use then reactivated]; L4.5
    CIVILIZATIONAL TRANSMISSION THRESHOLD [tempered -- no one-generation ceiling
    rise unlike [CASE:SEQUOYAH_SYLLABARY]].

  CAUSAL LEGIBILITY VIA DESIGN TREATISE [primary proof]:
    Hangul is the only major writing system with a known inventor and a
    contemporaneous published design rationale surviving readable today [desk
    conjunct when scoped to major national spoken-language scripts with royal
    commission + Haerye-class treatise]. Hunminjeongeum Haerye [c. Oct 1446]
    documents why each letter looks as it does: basic consonants are stylised
    articulatory diagrams [ㄱ velar, ㄴ dental, ㅁ bilabial, ㅅ dental fricative,
    ㅇ glottal]; vowels encode Confucian Heaven/Earth/Human cosmology with
    combinatorial vowel-harmony structure. Sejong preface states egalitarian
    intent: Chinese characters structurally unsuited to Korean phonology;
    literacy restricted to elite who could afford years of Hanja study. The
    treatise makes the design mechanism legible to skeptical instruments --
    contrast [CASE:SEQUOYAH_SYLLABARY] [process known; shapes not treatise-
    explained] and [CASE:SEQUOYAH_SYLLABARY] falsifier shelf Braille [inventor
    + 1829 treatise but not major national script scope without analyst gate].
    Sequoyah falsifies bare inventor absolutism but not inventor+rationale
    conjunct [no Haerye-class document].

  MINIMAL UNIFICATION [confirmed]:
    Jamo featural alphabet + syllable-block assembly [가, 한, 글] collapses
    Chinese logographic burden for Korean phonology -- same structural move
    class as Sequoyah syllable collapse [sparse anchor chosen over vocabulary-
    first]. Learnability claim [wise man morning / fool ten days] is intent-
    signal; adoption speed is separate variable [see DELAYED ADOPTION below].

  NAMED SUB-FINDING: DELAYED PRIMARY SCRIPT ADOPTION:
    Hangul did not become primary script rapidly post-1446. Choe Manri memorial
    1444 objects on Confucian hierarchy grounds: non-Chinese script diminishes
    Korea's cultural status; widespread lower-class literacy threatens yangban
    monopoly on written knowledge. Elite continued Hanja for serious literary
    and bureaucratic use for ~450 years. Hangul used by women [amgeul], lesser
    Buddhist orders, popular novelists -- vernacular stigma lane. Official
    primary-script permission Gabo Reform 1894; colonial-era national-identity
    revival; post-1945 sole script both Koreas. **Desk confirms delay** --
    falsifies overstated one-generation civilizational literacy ceiling read.
    Nationalism channel and colonial stress test adoption arc; not automatic
    structural literacy gain from design alone.

  NAMED SUB-FINDING: L4.5 TEMPERED [contrast Sequoyah]:
    Cherokee syllabary raised transmission ceiling in ~one generation [~5 years
    to majority literacy; Cherokee Phoenix 1828]. Hangul design survived
    centuries of non-primary use then reactivated under stress [colonial
    occupation; partition] -- transmission selectivity of a different tempo.
    No scored L4 methodology export equivalent to Vai via Curtis [Hangul
    propagation is script adoption not transferable syllabary-design methodology
    export]. L4.5 near-case label **tempered** not rejected.

  NAMED SUB-FINDING: ELITE LITERACY MONOPOLY RESISTANCE [Choe Manri 1444]:
    Behavioral record of elite blocking when design threatens exclusive control
    of correction-bearing text. Social compliance channel reads new script as
    cultural barbarism and hierarchy threat -- parallel class to Sequoyah
    witchcraft charge but institutional elite memorial not individual sorcery
    accusation.

  Extraction mechanisms active: elite Hanja monopoly [received -- yangban
    literacy gate]; colonial occupation [received -- identity-revival channel].
  T3 Medium on Sejong parallel mapping [well-documented history; framework
    labels analogies]. Medium-High on desk conjunct when scoped to Haerye-class
    rationale on major national scripts. Low on bare "only inventor"
    [Sequoyah + Braille falsifiers].

  FALSIFICATION: Adoption speed overstated vs elite resistance [desk confirms
    delay]; Hangul success read as nationalism-only without structural literacy

[CASE:LYSENKO]
[PRIMARY_Q:AXIOM CAPTURE]
Lysenkoism [Soviet Union -- 1948-1964; Trofim Lysenko; primary
  sources: Medvedev [The Rise and Fall of T.D. Lysenko, 1969;
  smuggled scientific document, peer-reviewed translation];
  Joravsky [The Lysenko Affair, 1970; Cambridge University Press];
  Soyfer [Lysenko and the Tragedy of Soviet Science, 1994; Rutgers
  University Press]; independent cross-confirmation across Soviet
  and Western scientific history scholarship]
  Type: Primary [S:3]
  Proof for: AXIOM CAPTURE [v25 -- primary case; L5 failure mode;
    rigorous methodology applied to installed axiom producing
    civilizational-scale damage].

  MECHANISM TRACE:
    The installed axiom: dialectical materialism as the self-
      evident foundation of correct biology. Genes cannot exist
      because genes are idealist bourgeois science. Inheritance
      of acquired characteristics must be true because it is
      consistent with materialist philosophy. The axiom was not
      subject to falsification by biological evidence -- it was
      the frame through which biological evidence was evaluated.
    The rigorous methodology: peer review within the accepted
      frame [Lysenkoist journals; Michurinist biology meetings],
      consistent application of axiom set to experimental data,
      genuine revision of specific findings within the frame
      [specific crop protocols adjusted based on results].
      Internal logic was coherent. Disagreeing scientists were
      expelled, imprisoned, or killed -- not because their
      evidence was examined and found wanting, but because
      they were questioning the axiom rather than working
      within it. The distinction between legitimate scientific
      disagreement and ideological deviation was the tell.
    What could not be questioned: the axiom itself.
      Medvedev [1969] documents that geneticists who published
      evidence for Mendelian inheritance were not refuted on
      evidentiary grounds -- they were removed from the frame.
      The correct response within Lysenkoism to disconfirming
      evidence was to question the experimenter, not the axiom.
    The outputs: agronomic recommendations derived rigorously
      from the installed axiom set. Cluster planting [derived
      from Lysenko's theory of interspecies cooperation],
      vernalization protocols, rejection of hybrid seed corn.
      All internally consistent with the axiom; all contributed
      to repeated crop failures and famine conditions. Joravsky
      and Soyfer both document the mechanism: the failure was
      not from sloppy science but from rigorous science applied
      to installed premises.
    The tell: the characteristic blind spot. Soviet biology had
      high resolution on many questions and zero tolerance for
      one class of question [genetic inheritance]. The domain
      blindness co-existing with genuine domain competence
      elsewhere is the Axiom Capture signature.

  AXIOM CAPTURE INSTRUMENT RUN [five conditions]:
    [1] METHODOLOGY GENUINELY RIGOROUS AT L4: confirmed.
      Internal review, consistent application, revision of
      specific findings within frame. Not sloppy science.
      Joravsky [1970]: Lysenko's methodology was internally
      coherent and consistently applied.
    [2] AXIOMS INSTALLED RATHER THAN EXAMINED: confirmed.
      Dialectical materialism as self-evident foundation.
      Not subject to falsification by biological evidence.
      Medvedev [1969]: the frame was immune to empirical
      challenge by institutional enforcement.
    [3] PRODUCES RIGOROUS CONCLUSIONS FROM CAPTURED BASE:
      confirmed. Agronomic recommendations derived correctly
      from the axiom set. Soyfer [1994]: the crop failure
      mechanism traced to the conclusions, which traced to
      the axioms, not to methodological errors within the
      frame.
    [4] CHARACTERISTIC BLIND SPOT: confirmed. Genetics as
      the domain that could not be examined. High competence
      in adjacent domains; zero tolerance in the one domain
      where the axiom was directly testable.
    [5] EXTERNAL CONFIRMATION: confirmed. Three independent
      scholarly accounts [Medvedev, Joravsky, Soyfer] across
      Soviet dissident and Western academic sources confirm
      the same mechanism trace. Cross-ideological confirmation.

  T3 Low: well-documented across independent peer-reviewed
    historical scholarship. No analyst prior activates on
    Soviet biology mechanism. The political dimension [Stalin
    endorsement] is contextual, not the mechanism -- the
    mechanism is Axiom Capture, which operates independent
    of political ideology. S:3.

  Cross-ref: AXIOM CAPTURE [weapon entry -- this is the
    primary S:3 confirmation case]; L5 [TRUTH AXIS AND
    CYCLES UNIFICATION -- L5 is what would have detected
    and corrected this; the axiom layer was uncorrectable
    within the system]; L4 MIMICRY [distinct -- Lysenkoism
    had genuine methodology, not fake meta-correction];
